### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 14 日(水)

1/3 
10 月の金融政策決定会合は利上げ見送りへ  
政策金利の２％超えは？ 
 経済調査部主席エコノミスト藤代宏一 
（℡：050-5474-6123）

・10月30日の金融政策決定会合に先立って、早くも観測記事が金融市場を賑わせている。米系
通信は「日銀は今月会合で利上げ見送る公算大、12月の可能性排除せず」と報じた。記事内
では「経済・物価は日銀の見通しに沿って推移しており、２会合連続での利上げが必要なほ
ど物価の上振れリスクが一段と強まっている情勢にはない」、とはいえ「一時的な要因を除
いた基調的な物価上昇率は上昇を続けており、目標の２％から上振れていくリスクには依然
として注意が必要だ」などとする日銀関係者の発言が記述されていた。また既報のとおり、
基調的物価上昇率が概ね２％に達したとの判断を展望レポートに記すことにも触れられてい
た。

・10月の利上げ見送りの根拠として、昨日発表された９月の企業物価指数は一定の存在感があ
る。国内企業物価指数は前月比▲0.3％、前年比＋6.8％と上昇が一服し、市場予想の同＋
7.6％も下回った。原油価格が高止まりするなか、世界的なＡＩ・半導体関連需要の拡大など
を背景に非鉄金属や化学製品などで上昇が続いた一方、米を中心とする農林水産物の価格下
落が下押しに寄与した。円ベースの輸入物価指数は前年比＋24.5％と大幅な上昇を記録した
・日経平均株価は先行き12 ヶ月72,000 円程度で推移するだろう 
・USD/JPY は先行き12 ヶ月 155 円程度で推移するだろう 
・日銀は政策金利を12 月に 1.5％とした後、27 年末までに2.25％とするだろう 
・FED はFF 金利を、年内は4.0％で据え置くだろう

### Page 2

2/3 
が、８月の同＋25.1％からは小幅に鈍化した。川上段階のインフレ圧力は依然として強いも
のの、９月会合時点の判断に大きな修正を迫るものではなかったと考えられる。

・同日発表された「生活意識に関するアンケート調査」（９月調査）も似たような結果であっ
た。５年後の物価予想については「かなり上がる」の割合が51.1％と前回調査対比で概ね横
ばいとなり、「少し上がる」との合計では86.5％と高止まりした。他方、今後５年間の物価
上昇率（年平均）は、中央値が５％で横ばいも、平均値は＋10.1％と前回調査対比で0.7％pt
低下した。ガソリン代の補助に加え、米価の下落が効いたとみられ、家計の予想物価上昇率
が一段と上放れする事態は回避された。政府による家計支援策が効いているとはいえ、家計
の予想物価上昇率が低下したことは、日銀に時間的余裕を与え得る。なお、景況感について
は「１年前との比較」と「１年後の予想」の双方とも大きな変化は認められなかった。

### Page 3

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
3/3 
・先行きの金融政策はどうなるであろうか。筆者は、10月会合は利上げ見送り、12月会合で利
上げを決定し、政策金利は1.5％で越年すると予想する。Ｆｅｄの利上げ観測が燻り、円安圧
力が強いなか、日銀が金融引き締め方向への手を緩めるとなれば、一段と円安が進行しかね
ない。また日銀短観（９月調査）を見る限り、企業の金融環境に大きな変化はみられず、既
往の利上げが実体経済に染み渡っている様子はそれほど窺えない。これらを踏まえ、筆者は
2027年も３～４回の利上げがあり、政策金利は２％を超えることを想定している。

・ただし、Ｆｅｄが2027年に政策金利を据え置き、その次の政策変更が利下げになるとの見方
が生じれば、日銀は円安対策から解放され、利上げを中断ないしは終了する可能性がある。
その点、ウィリアムズ・ＮＹ連銀総裁とウォラー理事の発言は注視しておきたい。まずウィ
リアムズ総裁は10月13日に「来年はインフレがかなり鈍化し、通年でみれば、全体のインフ
レ率は目標⁠の２％近辺になるとみている。これは私が言及した要因、すなわち関税の影響が
完全に過去のものとなることを反映している」として、2027年に利上げは必要ないとの見方
を示した。

・⁠ウォラー理事は10月13日に「最近の米国債利回りの上昇は経済成長の加速とＡＩへの大規模
投資によるもので、インフレ期待の高まりが原因ではない」、「インフレ期待は引き続き
『しっかりと安定している』」と述べ、その上で「データセンター建設を含むＡＩ投資が経
済の需要を支えており、今後２～３年はその効果が続く⁠可能性がある。大幅な生産性向上が
実現するのは、なお『数年先』だ」とした。同理事の発言を整理すると、①予想インフレは
安定しており、その点において現在の金融政策は適切である、②ＡＩ投資が一巡すれば、そ
れに起因するインフレ圧力は鈍化する、③その後に生産性向上によるディスインフレ方向へ
の動きが予想される、といったところであろう。

・両氏の予想が現実のものになれば、Ｆｅｄの利上げ観測は後退し、場合によっては2027年に
利下げ観測が台頭することもあり得る。そうした環境下、日銀は利上げペースを緩める、或
いは終了を検討するなど、多くの選択肢を手にすることができる。

Low CTE 則已於 2025 年 底 開 始 小 量 出 貨 ， 目 前 產 品 出 貨 量 已 較2026 年 初 顯 著 增 加 。

段， 公司 亦 有 望 部 分 反 映 報 價 。 給 予 投 資 建 議Buy ， 目 標 價 81 元。

A52320@cim.capital.com.tw

台 玻 首 戰 TPCA Show ：  
台玻 10/20/2026 首戰 台 灣 電 路 板 產 業 國 際 博 覽 會(TPCA Show 2026) 。本次
會中 主 要 展 出 其 高 階 玻 纖 布 ， 包 括 TD(Low Dk1) 、 TDA(Low Dk2) 、 TS(Low 
CTE) 、 Q (Q 布 )等 ， 並 詳 解 各 產 品 特 性 及 運 用 範 疇 。 
一、 聚 焦 於 Low Dk 、 Low CTE 玻纖布 ：  
TD(Low Dk1) 、 TDA(Low Dk2) 具 低 介 電 損 耗 並 於 維 持 一 定 電 性 水 準 下 ， 提
供 較 佳 產 品 穩 定 性 。 目 前 主 要 應 用 於 電 子 產 業 及PCB 產 業 、IC 載 板 、 先 進
封 裝 材 料 、 航 空 及 汽 車 領 域 等 。 
TS(Low CTE) 具 低 熱 膨 脹 係 數 特 性， 並 具 備 高機 械 韌 性(剛性 )、 絕 佳 電 性 絕
緣 及 先 進 樹 酯兼 容 性。目 前 主 要 應 用 於 5G/6G 、微 波 通 訊 設 備、高 速 電 路 板、
高頻 PCB 、 光 模 塊 、 高 速 交 換 器 及資 料 傳 輸 等。  
二 、 目 前 營 收 比 重 為 Low Dk2>Low Dk1=Low CTE ：  
E 布 轉 至 中 國 產 銷 ，以 Low Dk 1/Dk2 為 當 前 玻 纖 布 事 業 群 主 要 營 收來 源 。
Low CTE 則 已於 2025 年 底 開 始 小 量 出 貨 ， 目 前產 品 出 貨 量 已 較 2026 年初
顯 著 增 加，預 期 營 收 佔 比 將 於 2027 年 陸 續 提 升，並 於 2028 年 伴隨 近 年 陸 續
擴建 之 新 產 能 開 出 後 ，顯 著 提 高 。鑒 於 目 前 下 游 終 端 需 求 仍 以 Low Dk 產品
為主力， 研究部 預期 2027 年 Low Dk2 營 收 比 重 仍 將 高 於 Low Dk1 及 Low 
CTE 。 當 前 營 收 比 重 為 Low Dk2>Low Dk1=Low CTE 。 另 外 ， 台 玻 目 前 亦嘗
試 生 產 測 試 Low Dk3( 三代布 )， 後 續 若 生 產 良 率 過 關 ， 亦 將送 樣 驗 證 。 
三 、 不 急 於 產 品 漲 價 ， 堅 守 優 質 客 戶 連 結：  
台 玻 生 產 研 發 玻 纖 布數 十 年，始 終 秉 持 於 維 繫 良 好 客 戶 關 係，並 以 客 戶 需 求
為 營 運 宗 旨 。 雖 2025~2026 年以來 E 布 報 價 倍 數 增 長 ， 惟 基 於多年來與 客
戶 合作 建立 之 信任 及當前產品利潤貢獻尚屬可控範圍，故而 預期將 不急於
大 幅 反 映 產 品 報 價 ， 但 亦 不 排 除 部 分 調 整 產 品 報 價 。 
四 、 具 備 Q 布 生 產 技 術 及 能 力：  
台 玻 於 本 次 TPCA Show 上 ， 已 展 出 Q 布 玻 纖 紗 ，顯示 其 Q 布 技 術 儲 備 ， 且
已 具 備 接 單 出 貨 能 力 。 惟 觀 察 AI 伺 服 器 規 格 調 整，預期 2027 年 產 品 需 求
仍以 Low Dk 系 列 玻 纖 布 為 主 ， 大 量 搭 載 Q 布 尚 早 。 不 同 材 料 規 格 之 玻 纖
布 ， 其 製 程 方 式雖 雷 同 ， 惟 配 方 均 有 差 異 ，窯 爐 使 用 亦 不 同 。 因 此 ， 台 玻 將
視 下 游 廠 商 產 品 需 求 ， 決 議 Q 布 生 產 時 程。

台玻 09/2026 營收 49.68 億 元 ，MoM+8.18% 、YoY+34.11% ，主因 Low Dk1/Low 
Dk2 等 高 階 玻 纖 布 出 貨 動 能 較 08/2026 提 升。若 以 玻 纖 布 營 收 比 重 40~45%，
且高階玻纖布佔其中 40~42% 推估， 09/2026 高階玻纖布營收貢獻約
MoM+7~9% 。 3Q26 營收 合計 約 141.41 億 元 ， QoQ+17.24% 、 YoY+31.82% ，
亦 優 於 原 預 期 之 138.80 億元， QoQ+15.08% 、 YoY+29.40% 。  
展望 2027 ~2028 年 ， 預 期 2027 年 Low Dk1/Dk2 玻 纖 布 出 貨 量 仍 將 較 2026
年 顯 著 提 升，Low CTE 營 收 貢 獻 亦 將 持 續 走 揚，2028 年 伴 隨 新 產 能 開 出 後，
有望趕上 Low Dk2 。 目 前 台 玻 Low CTE 產 品 已 陸 續 取 得 國 內 外 大 廠 認 證 ，
訂 單 能 見 度 亦 已 達 2027 年，且 2028 年 訂 單 洽 談 順 暢。因 此，供 應 鏈 產 品 需
求若 無 較 大 變 化 ，2028 年 玻 纖 布 事 業 群 仍 將持 續 成 長 。

非 當 前 公 司 擴 產 速 度 所 能 消 化 ， 顯 示 產 品 需 求 強 勁 。 研 究 部預 估 台 玻 4Q26
底 ~2027 年 玻 纖 布 營 收 比 重 有 望 超 越 平 板 玻 璃 和 玻 璃 器 皿 。預估 2027 年營

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

完善稳定机制，深化投融资改革——2026 金融
街论坛年会

报告日期 2026-10-22 专题报告 
  ⚫ 央行完善流动性支持。拟设非银机构大额赎回流动性支持
工具，扩大 SLF 适用范围，并推进利率框架改革，改善市

市、并购重组和再融资改革，支持企业发展。

期、改善配置条件，后续重点关注权益资金持续流入，以
及盈利改善向更多行业和成分股扩散。

交易咨询业务资格：证监许可【2012】31 号

一、2026金融街论坛年会 
2026 金融街论坛年会于 10 月 19 日在北京金融街开幕，会期为 10 月 19 日至 22 日，由北
京市人民政府与中国人民银行、国家金融监督管理总局、中国证监会、新华通讯社、国家外
汇管理局共同主办，主题为“开放·合作·包容——共筑全球金融发展新生态” 。 
本届年会采用“主论坛 +平行论坛+专题大会+配套活动”架构，包含主论坛、平行论坛
及三场专题大会：金融科技大会聚焦智能经济；保险大会首次由金融监管总局主办，聚焦保
险业高质量发展，并拟发布相关研究成果；投资大会聚焦跨境投资与资本对接。年会首次将
分会场拓展至非洲，在肯尼亚内罗毕设置分会场，并安排北京、香港、伦敦三地连线。四部
门主要负责人出席开幕式并释放重要政策信号。论坛兼具政策发布、行业交流、国际合作和
投融资对接功能。 
二、会议解读 
本次论坛上，央行、金融监管总局、证监会、外汇局负责人先后发言，政策信号主要体
现为：完善金融市场稳定机制，同时改善资金参与资本市场的制度条件。前者重在防止流动
性冲击被放大，后者重在提高资金配置的稳定性和效率，两者有利于改善市场运行基础。 
1、央行行长潘功胜：完善流动性支持，提高利率传导效率 
央行提出设立应对非银机构大额赎回的流动性支持工具。当资管机构面临群体性流动性
压力时，产品集中赎回可能迫使机构出售债券，债券价格下跌又会压低产品净值，诱发更多
赎回，形成负反馈。新工具对此提供应对机制，旨在防止债券市场的流动性冲击扩大并向其
他市场传递。工具在特定压力情景下启用，操作须满足宏观审慎要求、提供高等级抵押品。
此外，扩大常备借贷便利（ SLF）适用范围，重在完善银行体系的临时流动性支持渠道；推
动银行间与交易所债券市场互联互通，有助于改善跨市场交易、流动性配置和价格形成。 
利率框架改革是另一条重要线索，涉及利率调控与贷款定价的“基准体系” 。具体包括三
个方面：一是研究择机将公开市场主要操作品种的期限由 7 天切换为隔夜，使公开市场隔夜
回购利率相应成为主要政策利率，提高利率调控的精准性；二是推动贷款定价基准多元化，
未来随着相关机制完善，财务能力强的企业可与金融机构协商，自主选择 DR001 或 LPR 作为

### Page 3

交易咨询业务资格：证监许可【2012】31 号

浮动利率贷款定价基准，逐步拓宽 LPR 以外的定价选择；三是推动原存贷款基准利率退出。
该利率自 2015 年 10 月以来长期未作调整，目前在存贷款定价中已不再发挥作用，下一步将
推动司法等少数领域的计息依据改用市场化利率指标。 
2、证监会主席吴清：完善稳市机制，推进投融资改革和市场治理 
吴清的讲话围绕市场稳定、包容性改革、市场生态和对外开放展开。投资端重点是健全
长周期考核机制、拓宽中长期资金入市渠道、深化公募基金改革，并宣布证监会当日将同意
首批 18 只主动 ETF 注册。同时，鼓励有条件的上市公司加大回购增持力度。这些安排分别作
用于机构考核、产品供给和股东回报，有助于提高权益配置的稳定性。 
融资端重点是落实“两创板”改革，推进发行上市、并购重组和再融资制度调整，提高
资本市场对科技企业和不同发展阶段企业的适应性。同时，支持证券公司并购重组及后续整
合，提升投资机构服务能力；推进北证 50ETF、北交所新股市值配售等安排，完善服务创新
型中小企业的市场体系。 
市场治理方面，筹建基金业运行监测中心、规范人工智能在证券期货业的应用、完善违
规减持处罚规则和先行赔付制度，分别加强风险监测、技术治理、交易约束和投资者保护。
政策着重提高监管有效性和市场规则的可预期性，为长期投资提供制度保障。 
对外开放方面，提升 QFII 投资便利度，支持境内外机构开发更多中国资产相关产品，推
进人民币股票交易柜台和 REITs 纳入沪深港通，拓宽跨境投资渠道。同时，强调防范跨境跨
市场风险传导，关注境外挂钩 A 股个股的杠杆产品等新风险点。 
3、金融监管总局局长丁向群、外汇局局长朱鹤新：推进风险治理、
机构转型和跨境便利化 
金融监管总局强调在风险治理基础上推进机构转型和资源配置调整。丁向群提出，通过
兼并重组、市场退出等方式稳妥处置高风险机构，强化早期纠正和穿透监管，减少风险积累
及其传染。同时，在保障基础服务、考虑承载能力和保持适度竞争的条件下，因地制宜推进
中小机构减量提质，推动机构形成与自身能力相匹配的业务定位。 
金融服务结构调整同样重要。银行资金要更好适应先进制造业、服务业和基础设施建设
需要，保险则要增强灾害、航运、养老健康和科技创新等领域的保障能力。支持商业航天、

### Page 4

交易咨询业务资格：证监许可【2012】31 号

生物医药、智能机器人等领域专业共保体建设，说明科技金融不仅涉及提供融资，也涉及分
担研发和产业化风险。相关业务能否形成稳定盈利，取决于金融机构的专业服务、风险定价
和资本管理能力。 
外汇局重在提高企业跨境经营便利度，完善双向资金配置条件。朱鹤新介绍，拟推出的
增量措施涉及经常项目便利化、资本项目外汇管理优化，以及外汇市场开放和证券公司结售
汇业务试点。政策直接服务于企业跨境结算、投融资和汇率风险管理，重在降低业务办理成
本，提高资金配置效率。 
这一方向同时服务“引进来”和“走出去” 。结合央行关于人民币清算、离岸市场和流动
性供给的部署，相关政策有助于改善企业跨境经营和人民币资产配置的基础条件。对 A 股的
影响主要通过经营成本、融资条件和投资便利度传导。外资是否增加权益配置，仍取决于盈
利、估值、汇率和全球资产的相对吸引力。 
3、股指启发 
本届论坛的政策意义，主要在于稳定市场预期、改善长期投资条件，并支持企业融资与
经营。稳市机制和流动性支持，有助于减少集中赎回、被动抛售和跨市场风险传染，改善投
资者持有权益资产的信心；主动 ETF、投资便利化和长周期考核，有助于丰富配置渠道、减
少短期业绩压力对投资行为的干扰，回购增持则通过实际执行形成购买需求。这些政策对股
指的支持，需逐步落实为持续配置和盈利改善。 
对股指而言，长期投资条件改善，有利于盈利稳定、股东回报较好的企业获得配置支持 。
融资改革和科技金融则为相关企业研发、产业化和盈利增长提供条件。政策效果将通过资金
配置与企业经营逐步体现 。若盈利改善扩散至更多行业和成分股，并伴随持续资金流入，指
数修复基础将更稳固。若经营兑现偏慢或风险偏好回落，市场仍可能反复。

### Page 5

交易咨询业务资格：证监许可【2012】31 号

分析师介绍：

和宏观研究。曾获期货日报"最佳宏观金融期货分析师"称号。

股指与金融衍生品研究，擅长将机器学习、人工智能等量化技术应用于股指及金融衍生品研
究领域，构建套利、趋势跟踪等交易策略。

Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
What’s Changed
Nanya Technology Corp.
(2408.TW) From To
Price Target NT$580.00 NT$600.00
DRAM supply constraints continue. Contract renewals drove
Nanya's 3Q beat.  CXMT's G5 milestone strengthens its tech
roadmap, but yield ramp and customer qualification take time.

Nanya 3Q beat; LTAs in spotlight:  Nanya reported 3Q revenue of NT$134bn, +62%
Q/Q, +612% Y/Y, 8% above consensus. 3Q gross profit was NT$114bn, +114% Q/Q,
12% above consensus, implying a GM of 85.7%. 3Q net profit was NT$88bn, +75%
Q/Q, 9% above consensus, implying a net margin of 65.1%. The 3Q Q/Q revenue
growth was driven mainly by ASP increase, which primarily reflects renewal of the
one-year and six-month L TAs, which were previously negotiated when ASPs were

lower; most  expired in 3Q26. Currently L TAs cover 50-70% of output; only 20% of
output is covered by three-year contracts; Nanya intends to extend the L TA
durations for longer-term supply according to customer demand, and there are
discussions on price floors and ceilings for the new contracts.
Nanya management sees continued DRAM shortage in 4Q and expects that ASP
may continue to improve: The supply constraint is the long lead time for new
capacity, and the undersupply is unlikely to be resolved in the near term. Nanya

targets capex  of NT$69.7bn in 2026 and NT$346bn  for the 35.9kwpm capacity of its
new Fab 5A from 2026-29. Management estimates that AI infra currently accounts
for ~20% of its revenue mix, but that could increase to 40-50% by 2029/30.
CXMT updates: On September 7 during Q&A, CXMT reiterated expectations for
tight DRAM supply in 2H26; its response on Apple expressed openness to global
customers without confirming a supply agreement. On September 21, after its G5

mass production announcement, CXMT clarified that G5 sales are not yet at scale,
while yield ramp-up and customer qualification would take time. Based on our
checks, CXMT's HBM3E yield is already 40%, and DRAM wafer input should ramp to
30kwpm by end-2027.
Constrained DRAM supply is the industry consensus: The 4QF26 earnings call for
Micron  (covered by Joseph Moore) highlighted that  persistent supply constraints are
expected through CY2027-28. It cited industry-wide clean room constraints owing to

lengthy construction; most of the increase in Micron's F27 capex (vs. its previous
plan) is allocated to construction, targeting clean room availability in late CY28 and
beyond. Micron also expects rising industry HBM wafer requirements to limit bit
growth, forecasting industry DRAM bit shipment growth of low-20s (%) in CY27-28.
Samsung (covered by Shawn Kim) also expects HBM to absorb nearly 30% of
industry DRAM wafer capacity in 2027, vs ~20% currently.

Stock implications:
• Nanya Tech (OW):  Price target raised to NT$600; 2026e, 2027e, and 2028e
EPS raised  22%, 18%, and 19%, respectively.
•  CXMT (OW): Base case value/price target unchanged at Rmb88.

Nanya Tech: Valuation Methodology
We raise our price target (base case scenario value) from NT$580 to NT$600: Our
price target reflects a BVPS multiple of 4.11x for 2026 (was 4.77x) versus ROE of 76% for
2026.  We lower our target multiple to reflect recent memory segment volatility. Our
2026e BVPS estimates is raised to NT$146.00 from NT$121.52. We expect DDR4 pricing
strength and L TAs to drive a re-rating.
We raise our bull and bear case values from NT$1,230 and NT$320 to NT$1,270 and NT

CXMT: Valuation Methodology
We keep our base case value/price target unchanged: We continue to apply RI
methodology to derive our price target. The price target did not change despite our
2026/27 earnings estimate increases because the contribution to calculated value from
our RI model comes mostly from the subsequent forecast period  and the continuing value.
Key RI assumptions remain unchanged, including cost of equity of 9.9%, , intermediate

growth rate of 11.0%, terminal growth rate of 3.0%, and cash dividend payout ratio of 51%,
.
Our base case value/price target now implies 14.3x 2027e EPS vs. our prior target multiple
of 18.4x 2027e BVPS, as we see a stronger DRAM market outlook given the continued
supply constraint; we also believe the G5 breakthrough reduces risks around CXMT's
technology roadmap.
Our bull case value remains unchanged at Rmb132; it now implies 21.5x our 2027e EPS.

We adjust our bear case value to Rmb40 from Rmb70 to account for recent volatility; it
now implies 6.5x 2027e EPS.

Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions:

51% payout ratio
11.0% medium-term growth rate
3% terminal growth rate
We believe these assumptions are justiﬁed, given CXMT's fast growth, capacity expansion,
localization tailwinds and AI-memory exposure. Target implies 14.3x 2027e P/E vs global
peers' 4.4x 2027e P/E; the premium reﬂects CXMT's stronger growth proﬁle.

and a key beneﬁciary of China’s memory
localization push.

memory peers as justiﬁed by CXMT’s faster

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 2

Key Takeaways from Company Meetings

Key Takeaways from Applied Materials Meeting 
Participants: Kevin Moraes, VP , Strategy and Marketing; Sree Kesapragada, VP , 
Advanced Packaging; Mike Sullivan, Head of Investor Relations 
Investor questions mainly focused on clarifications tied to the company’s investor event 
and financial targets. On revenue and capital intensity, management noted that 
semiconductor industry pricing is a key variable that it cannot control - but has high 
confidence in its framework tied to increasing WFE intensity per node - and these 
increases have already been programmatically locked in by customers today. On 
Applied’s target of 200bps of market share gain, management noted the complex set of 
mix effects involved in the calculation, but noted the substantial impact from China 
export control exclusions. Relative to the company’s newly articulated gross margin 
target of 54% by 2030, Applied believes there is the potential for upside to this target - 
but pointed out that the vast majority of its costs are variable in nature. On its optical 
tool portfolio, Applied believes that it can leverage its expertise and market position in 
e-beam technology to expand into this adjacent $9bn market. On AGS, management has 
high confidence in its outlook for mid-teens long term revenue growth - but noted that 
its services attach rate is higher on its newer tools relative to its installed fleet. 
Key Takeaways from Lam Research Meeting 
Participants: Ram Ganesh - VP of Investor Relations 
Consistent with prior commentary, Lam expects 8-10 new fab projects to come online in 
2027 - and currently expects product growth to be driven by DRAM, followed by 
advanced foundry/logic, followed by NAND flash. The company has not specified a 
timeframe for achieving its new long-term gross margin target of ~55%. However, we 
expect this to be achieved over several years, and see it tied more to product mix 
(particularly with new products such as Akara) and overseas operational manufacturing 
efficiencies rather than like-for-like pricing. Management remains constructive on the 
company’s competitive positioning in Logic, DRAM, and advanced packaging - 
particularly as etch and deposition intensity continues to grow with a variety of new 
device structures coming into the mix - including hard masks, surface prep, TSVs, and 
other materials engineering steps. 
Key Takeaways from Teradyne Meeting 
Participants: Roy Chorev - VP & GM of Compute Test Division; Amy McAndrews - VP of 
Investor Relations 
Teradyne announced a strategic partnership with and investment in FormFactor at 
SEMICON West. Teradyne remains committed to an open ecosystem of probe card 
vendors, but believes Form’s strong position in silicon photonics warrants a closer 
relationship that enables Teradyne to commit additional engineering platform resources. 
Regarding its XPU business, Teradyne continues to expect to gain merchant GPU share in 
the long run - and noted that Rubin test times are nearly 2X Blackwell given the near 
doubling in transistor count. Teradyne is also pursuing the tester opportunity at Intel, 
which is focused on increasing its exposure to external vendors (currently using an 
14 October 2026   2
Goldman Sachs Americas Technology: SemiconductorsFor the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 3

internal solution) beginning with its foundry business, where Teradyne sees a
medium-term opportunity. In its memory business, Teradyne expects minimal impact
from customer de-specing of HBM as its demand is tied primarily to unit volume rather
than stack height. In co-packaged optics, Teradyne continues to see a very strong
product ramp opportunity, although given the early stages of development, there are
likely to be significant shifts between testing intensity at different insertions during the

manufacturing process.
Key Takeaways from Camtek Investor Meeting
Participants: Lior Aviram - Executive Chairman; Rafi Amit - CEO; Ramy Langer -
President & COO; Moshe Eisenberg - CFO; Eyal Segev - Marketing Director
We attended Camtek’s Investor Meeting at SEMICON. Our key takeaways are as follows:
Long-term financial targets: In conjunction with its investor meeting, Camtek n
pre-announced 3Q26 revenue of $160 mn –$161 mn (above its prior guidance),

guided to ~$180 mn for 4Q26, and provided an initial 2027 revenue outlook of $800
mn based on orders received to date. Management laid out a long-term revenue
target of $1.2 billion in about three years, with a gross margin target of 55%-56%
and operating margin target of 34%-36%.
Advanced packaging remains the key growth driver: Camtek stressed that n
advanced packaging remains its core business, accounting for ~80% of recent
orders, and expects advanced packaging wafer growth to run at about twice the

broader industry rate through 2031 (13% vs. 7% CAGR). Within advanced packaging,
the company expects the high-end of the market (which accounts for the vast
majority of its business) to grow at a ~24% CAGR through 2031, driven by AI, silicon
photonics, denser interconnects, and increasingly complex inspection and metrology
requirements.
Technology and product roadmap centered on inspection and specialized n
metrology: Camtek’s roadmap remains centered on its margin-accretive Hawk and

Eagle products for inspection (~85% of its 2026 mix), as well as its metrology
platforms. Separately, management also noted it is embedding AI into detection and
classification and plans to launch AI-enabled software in 2027 (which it attributed to
its Visual Layer acquisition).
M&A strategy focused on capability acquisition and strategic expansion: Camtek n
noted that it is pursuing both smaller acquisitions that add technology and/or talent
to its core business, as well as larger strategic transactions that broaden its offering,

while emphasizing that its $1.2 bn revenue target and financial model do not assume
meaningful M&A activity.
Key Takeaways from Onto Innovation Meeting
Participants: Brian Roberts - CFO; Sidney Ho - VP of Investor Relations
Onto remains very constructive on the demand outlook through 2H26 and heading into
2027 across a range of demand drivers - with growth likely to accelerate next year given
the growth of incremental market opportunities. In HBM, Onto believes its business is

likely to be minimally impacted by any “de-spec” activity by XPU vendors, as its tool
14 October 2026   3
Goldman Sachs Americas Technology: SemiconductorsFor the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 4

demand is far more driven by unit volume than stack height. The company continues to
see CoWoS as a key demand driver that is increasingly driven by OSAT partners rather
than TSMC; Onto sees EMIB-T as a significant opportunity that promises to be material in
2028. From a competitive standpoint, Onto remains highly confident in its market share
position, including at TSMC - and sees no imminent impact from potential product
launches by competitors. On margins, management remains confident in its target of

350bps of gross margin expansion, which it believes can be achieved solely by product
mix and efficiencies in its manufacturing operations. The company believes it has room
to grow revenue substantially from projected levels, and does not see itself as capacity
constrained.
Key Takeaways from MKS Instruments Meeting
Participants: John Lee - President & CEO; Paretosh Misra - VP of Investor Relations
MKS believes the industry is in the early-to-middle stages of a substantial cyclical

industry upturn driven by AI and datacenter demand. The company is currently
preparing for a potential WFE spending envelope of up to $300bn in 2028, and
potentially higher over time. In the near-term, management expects the company to
potentially outgrow its customers (2Q26 was the first time the company delivered Y/Y
Semiconductor revenue growth in excess of them) given supply chain inventory effects.
MKS believes it has enough capacity to support WFE of ~$250bn with its Penang facility

(with a surge capacity of another 20%), but may need to expand capacity further if
these forecasts come to fruition. Importantly, the company does not believe it is
constraining its customers’ growth plans today. MKS has not taken significant pricing
actions, but could do so selectively in order to capture the value it delivers. Management
believes its product portfolio is well positioned, but would consider a possible
divestiture of its specialty industrial product lines at an attractive valuation.

### Page 5

Valuation/Risks

AMAT (Buy, on CL): Our 12-month price target of $670 is based on a 32X P/E multiple 
applied to our normalized EPS estimate of $21.00. Key downside risks include: (1) 
incremental export restrictions; (2) local China-based supplier share gains. 
LRCX (Buy): Our price target is $380, which is based on a 35X multiple applied to our 
normalized EPS forecast of $10.75. Key downside risks: (1) softer DRAM/HBM demand; 
(2) delays in planned clean room and fab expansions; (3) lower-than-expected content 
gains from advanced packaging and 3D architectures. 
TER (Buy): Our 12-month price target is $465, which is based on a 37X P/E multiple 
applied to our normalized EPS forecast of $12.50. Key downside risks: (1) delays in the 
revenue ramp with a merchant GPU customer, (2) significant quarterly volatility due to 
the lumpy nature of large AI-related infrastructure projects, (3) slower-than-expected 
recovery in Mobile. 
ONTO (Buy): Our12-month price target is $400, which is based on 32X our normalized 
EPS estimate of $12.50. Our target P/E is in line with peer multiples, and it is on the 
higher end of ONTO’s historical median, given recent momentum around HPC. Key 
upside/downside risks: (1) additional volume purchase agreements and accelerated 
customer adoption of Dragonfly G5; (2) shift towards OSATs or domestic Chinese 
manufacturers; (3) competitor launching a rival tool. 
CAMT (Neutral): Our 12-m price target of $160 is based on a 38X P/E multiple applied 
to our normalized EPS of $4.20. Key upside/downside risks: (1) notable push-outs in 
chiplet architectures; (2) failure to pass on higher pricing; (3) better-than-expected 
growth in HPC-driven revenue in the medium-term. 
MKSI (Sell): Our 12-month price target is $270, which is based on a 20X P/E multiple 
applied to our normalized EPS of $13.50. Key upside risks: (1) rebound in the NAND 
market from an equipment ordering perspective; (2) greater realization of synergies 
from the Atotech acquisition; (3) any material pickup in momentum for the company’s 
Semiconductor business due to broader WFE trends.

### Page 4

BT substrate supply/demand gap at 11%/14% in 2027/28E on the back of solid memory demand

We introduce our 2026-28E BT substrate TAM forecast, expanding at a 32% 2026-28E 
CAGR to reach US$16.8bn in 2028E (up from US$7.4bn in 2025; Exhibit 1). This growth is 
primarily driven by solid memory demand in general servers (general servers will account 
for 32% of total BT TAM in 2028E, up from 21% in 2025; Exhibit 2), with increasing 
memory capacity/BT content per server. We expect the memory BT substrate TAM to 
outpace the broader BT substrate TAM with a 41% 2026-28E CAGR, accounting for 77% 
of total BT substrate TAM in 2028E (was 56% in 2025). 
On the other hand, we expect the capacity growth rate to decelerate vs. the previous 
upcycle in 2020-22 (6% 2026-28E CAGR vs. 9% 2020-22 CAGR), as key Asian substrate 
suppliers are placing greater focus on their ABF substrate business, and are hence 
scaling back their BT substrate capacity (including Unimicron, which held 8% market 
share in 2025). 
As such, we now expect the overall BT substrate supply/demand gap to reach 
1%/11%/14% in 2H26/2027/2028 (Exhibit 3), while major BT suppliers have started to 
reduce capacity from 2026, potentially leading to a supply shortage in 2H26. We expect 
BT capacity utilization rates (UTR) to trend up amid a supply-constrained environment 
(surpassing 100% from 2027E per GSe), providing a better BT pricing environment going 
forward. The BT substrate supply tightness since the start of the year has directly led to 
a 10% YTD increase in overall BT substrate pricing, and we believe pricing momentum 
for BT substrates will remain robust in 2H26-2028E. 
Accordingly, we forecast overall BT substrate prices will further increase by 
15%-20%/10%-15% in 2027/28E, benefiting BT substrates’ profitability/pricing for 
Taiwanese players (GMs ranged from 15%-27% in 2Q26 and are projected to increase to 
18%-34%/19%-36%+ in 2027/28E; Exhibit 4).

AI; and (2) upcoming launches of new general server CPU platforms, which will drive

In terms of key end applications (Exhibit 6), we expect smartphone BT substrate TAM to 
grow at a 24% 2026-28E CAGR, accounting for 39% of total BT substrate TAM in 2028E 
(down from 51% in 2025; Exhibit 7). We expect general server BT substrate TAM to grow 
at a faster 42% 2026-28E CAGR, accounting for 32% of total BT substrate TAM in 2028E

faster growth among all applications at a 73% 2026-28E CAGR, reaching 9% of total BT 
substrate TAM in 2028E (up from 2% in 2025).

Solid growth in general server BT substrate TAM will support mid- to long-term BT 
market growth 
We expect general server BT substrate TAM to deliver a 42% 2026-28E CAGR and reach 
US$5.3bn in 2028E (up from US$1.6bn in 2025; Exhibit 8), which will account for 32% of 
total BT substrate TAM in 2028E (up from 21% in 2025). We expect general server 
memory BT substrate TAM to grow slightly faster at a 43% 2026-28E CAGR and reach 
US$5.0bn in 2028E (up from US$1.4bn in 2025), accounting for 39% of total memory BT 
substrate TAM in 2028E (up from 20% in 2025). This is driven by solid general server 
shipments (we expect general server shipments to register an 8% 2026-28E CAGR; see 
here; Exhibit 9) alongside increasing memory capacity/BT content per general server 
(Exhibit 10). 
Our US analyst, James Schneider, expects Intel/AMD general server CPU shipment to 
grow at a 28%/36% 2026-28E CAGR, reaching a combined 59mn units in 2028E (vs. 
26mn units in 2025). This supports our constructive view that general servers are the key 
driver of the current BT substrate upcycle, given the strong correlation between memory 
BT substrate TAM growth and general server CPU shipment growth.

### Page 8

Strong AI server shipment growth in 2026-28E will also support BT TAM expansion 
We expect AI server BT substrate TAM to deliver a 73% 2026-28E CAGR and reach 
US$1,476mn in 2028E (up from US$160mn in 2025; Exhibit 14), accounting for 9% of 
total BT substrate TAM in 2028E (up from 2% in 2025). We expect AI server memory BT 
substrate TAM to grow slightly faster at a 75% 2026-28E CAGR and reach US$1,428mn 
in 2028E (up from US$145mn in 2025), accounting for 9% of total memory BT substrate 
TAM in 2028E (up from 2% in 2025). This is driven by solid growth in AI server shipments, 
as we expect AI servers (8-GPU equivalent) and AI server racks (72-GPU equivalent) to 
register 17%/71% 2026-28E CAGRs (see here) with increasing memory capacity per 
server/per server rack. 
We expect GPU/CPU attach rates to move from 2x to 1.1-1.4x over time as agentic AI 
applications become mainstream, implying that each GPU deployment will require more 
server CPUs (see our US analyst Jim Schneider’s Intel initiation). Given that memory BT 
content in AI servers is primarily tied to server CPU count, if the GPU/CPU attach rate 
narrows from 2.0x to 1.1-1.4x, we estimate 1%/1%-2% upside to memory BT substrate 
TAM in 2027/28E. 
For AI server racks (72-GPU equivalent), BT content per rack continues to expand mainly 
due to increasing demand for more memory capacity, resulting in a 50% 2026-28E 
CAGR in BT content per AI server rack (Exhibit 15). A key upgrade is that Nvidia’s VR200 
will support higher DRAM capacity, implying a 100% increase in DRAM memory BT 
substrate content compared to GB200/GB300. We also note Nvidia is releasing a 
standalone Vera CPU rack designed for agentic AI, implying further demand upside for 
AI CPU shipments and memory BT substrates, as each Vera CPU rack incorporates 8x 
more DRAM memory BT substrate content compared to VR200.

grow over coming years which will bode well for BT market

### Page 9

Smartphones remain critical to BT demand with growth going forward tracking 
slower than the overall BT industry 
We expect overall smartphone BT substrate TAM to grow at a 24% 2026-28E CAGR 
(tracking slower than the overall BT industry CAGR of 32%) to reach US$6.5bn in 2028E 
(up from US$3.8bn in 2025), accounting for 39% of total 2028E BT TAM (down from 
51% in 2025). We expect smartphone memory BT substrate TAM to grow at a faster 36% 
2026-28E CAGR to reach US$2.4bn in 2028E (up from US$1.0bn in 2025), accounting 
for 19% of total memory BT TAM in 2028E (down from 24% in 2025). 
As mentioned in our previous update on the BT substrate industry (see here), we believe 
the previous upcycle (in 2020-22) was mainly driven by the proliferation of SiP/AiP 
designs that increased BT substrate content per device, especially for smartphones. 
While smartphones remain critical to overall BT demand, accounting for 51% of total 
2025 BT substrate TAM, we expect their contribution to moderate going forward 
(reaching 39% of 2028E BT TAM). This is driven by softer smartphone shipment volumes 
(we now forecast smartphone shipments at -10%/+2%/+2% YoY in 2026-28E; see here) 
and the fact that BT substrate content per smartphone will be mainly driven by higher 
memory BT substrate pricing (Exhibit 16) rather than other meaningful content value 
gains (memory will account for 46% of smartphone BT ASP in 2028E, up from 32% in 
2025). 
We expect 25%/26%/28% of overall smartphone shipments to be equipped with 
mmWave connectivity in 2026/27/28E (Exhibit 17), resulting in an 18% 2026-28E CAGR 
for smartphone AiP BT TAM to reach US$1,128mn in 2028 (up from US$713mn in 2025). 
For iPhones, mmWave connectivity is only available for models sold in the US 
(representing 35%-40% of total iPhone shipments per GSe). In the Android camp, 
Samsung’s flagship smartphones that support mmWave connectivity are also only for 
the US market.

### Page 10

Automotive BT consumption to steadily increase in coming years 
We expect automotive BT TAM to grow at a 34% 2026-28E CAGR and reach US$777mn 
in 2028E (up from US$317mn in 2025), which will account for 5% of total 2028E BT TAM 
(up from 4% in 2025). This stronger-than-industry growth is mainly driven by rising EV 
penetration going forward (EV penetration rate will increase from 14% in 2025 to 20% in 
2028E per our Japanese auto team; Exhibit 19) and the corresponding increase in 
electronics content per car, which requires more BT substrates. Furthermore, we believe 
rising autonomous driving penetration will result in an increase in electronic 
components/BT consumption per car.

BT market revenue to grow at a 28% 2026-28E CAGR with sustainable memory 
demand 
We project BT substrate market revenue to grow at a 28% CAGR in 2026-28E and reach 
US$14.6bn in 2028E (up from US$6.9bn in 2025). Despite this solid growth, total BT 
market revenue will account for less than 1% of customers’ overall revenue in 2026-28E 
(down from 2%-3% in the previous upcycle in 2020-22), as customers’ overall growth is 
tracking faster at a 34% 2026-28E CAGR. We believe this lower ratio of BT market

revenue to customers’ revenue is positive for BT suppliers, as it implies room for further

BT market revenue as a percentage of total customers’ revenue peaked during the 
2020-22 upcycle (at 2.5%-2.6%), when demand was primarily driven by smartphones 
(smartphones accounted for ~50% of total BT substrate demand during that cycle). As 
customers’ revenue has shifted away from consumer electronics toward AI-related 
applications, substrate spending has become increasingly directed toward ABF 
substrates for GPUs/CPUs/ASICs, while BT substrate spending is more focused on 
lower-end BT substrates for memory applications.

BT supply analysis: Major suppliers starting to reduce capacity, leading to slower capacity growth vs. 
previous upcycle

We believe overall BT industry supply growth in this upcycle will remain modest, as key 
Asian BT substrate suppliers are decreasing capacity, which will partially offset capacity 
additions from smaller players. Thus, we expect supply to grow at a 6% 2026-28E

substrates (see here), while SEMCO is focusing more on expanding its ABF substrate 
capacity (see here) with its BT substrate business mainly focusing on memory and

### Page 14

We have seen BT substrate demand improve since the start of 2026 alongside a firmer 
pricing outlook, and we expect demand growth to consistently outpace supply in 
2026-28E (demand CAGR of 15% vs. supply CAGR of 6%). We believe industry utilization 
rates (UTR) will continue to trend up over the coming years (reaching 100%+ after 
2027E) due to slow supply growth going forward. 
Memory predominantly requires wire bonding chip scale package (WBCSP) technology, 
and this production capacity is not interchangeable with flip-chip chip-scale package 
(FCCSP) technology (where smartphone APs represent the biggest demand driver). As 
such, we believe memory BT substrate supply will remain tight through at least 1H28, as 
new dedicated memory capacity will only come online in 2028. Overall BT pricing has 
already risen by 10% YTD, partially driven by higher material costs (including substrate 
CCL, copper foil, gold, etc., which account for 60% of total BT substrate BOM; Exhibit 
26). Going forward, we believe the slow increase in BT substrate supply, especially 
WBCSP capacity, will be the key price hike driver rather than simply reflecting the 
material cost increase. Furthermore, BT substrates typically account for less than 5% of 
total memory BOM, providing ample room for further BT price hikes amid the rising 
memory pricing trend.

### Page 15

With favorable BT substrate supply/demand conditions, we believe the profitability of 
key BT vendors will see a structural increase in the coming years. Similar to current 
dynamics in the ABF market, the BT substrate industry’s profitability profile has 
navigated three upcycles and three downcycles over the past 20 years (Exhibit 27). 
Overall margin profiles for key BT vendors have begun improving since the start of 2026, 
supported by increasing memory demand amid a supply-constrained environment, 
especially following several years of slow capex investment. We believe this profitability 
enhancement will continue in 2H26-2028, supported by sustained industry demand 
uptrend.

### Page 16

Optical module mSAP is seeing a widening S/D gap, prompting a shift of BT capacity to meet strong 
demand

We introduce our 2026-28E 800G+ optical module mSAP TAM forecast, expanding at an 
87% 2026-28E CAGR to reach US$7.1bn by 2028E (up from US$0.5bn in 2025; Exhibit 
28). This is based on our 800G+ optical module shipment estimates of a 48% 2026-28E 
CAGR (see here). The connectivity PCB within the 800G+ optical module requires mSAP 
technology, where several PCB makers have exposure to and are able to leverage their 
extensive mSAP experience from smartphone substrate-like PCB (SLP) production. 
On the other hand, major PCB and substrate suppliers are placing greater focus on this 
emerging business and pursuing proactive capacity expansion plans, especially ZDT and 
Compeq (the latter aims to ramp optical module mSAP capacity to reach monthly 
revenue of ~NT$3bn by end 2027 vs. less than NT$100mn as of 2Q26; see here). 
Although Unimicron was a front-runner in the 800G+ optical module mSAP business in 
2025 (29% market share in 2025 per GSe), the company is placing greater focus on its 
ABF substrate business, and hence leaving room for emerging peers to capture market 
share through aggressive expansion plans and technology leadership. 
We believe ZDT will become the market share leader in the optical module mSAP 
industry, as we expect the company to expand mSAP capacity by 5x over the next two 
years, positioning it well to gain share in a supply-constrained environment (our industry 
checks indicate mSAP is the most supply-constrained component in the 1.6T optical 
transceiver supply chain). Furthermore, the company currently holds an industry-leading 
yield of 90%+ for its optical module mSAP products. 
Overall, we expect the 800G+ optical module mSAP supply/demand gap to reach 
5%/25%/38% in 2026/27/28E (Exhibit 29). Although major mSAP suppliers continue 
to have proactive expansion plans, this appears insufficient to keep pace with strong 
demand. The 800G+ optical module mSAP tightness since the start of 2026 has directly 
led to a 20%-30% pricing increase YTD, and we believe the pricing outlook for 800G+ 
optical module mSAP will remain solid in 2027/28E.

### Page 23

Investment thesis, PT methodology and risks

Investment Thesis - Zhen Ding Technology 
Zhen Ding Technology (ZDT) is the global leading PCB supplier (top 1 in market share in 
sales) with 7-10% market share, and started its ABF substrate business from 2023 in 
Shenzhen, China; it has the highest ABF revenue exposure to the China market compared 
to all other ABF substrate suppliers. We believe ZDT will hold more than 40% ABF 
substrate market share in China by 2027E (up from 31% in 2024) and benefit the most 
from the strong and growing China ABF substrate market (53% 2025-27E CAGR) due to 
the increasing in-sourcing ratio for the AI computing ASIC/GPU market in China. We also 
expect the increasing foldable smartphone penetration rate in the long term to drive 
ZDT’s FPC TAM, and account for 39% of the company’s total revenue in 2027E. We see 
further revenue upside potential from foldable phones. We are Buy rated mainly due to 
our positive view on the company’s China ABF substrate business opportunity, which 
should not only drive ZDT’s revenue growth, but also improve its OPM level in the long 
term. We see upside for the stock price considering the company’s solid growth 
opportunity, and our TP implied P/E multiple is still lower than the company’s past 10+ 
year P/E average, which we believe is fair considering the potential upside from China 
ABF substrate market and increasing foldable phone penetration rate.  
Valuation: Our 12-month TP of NT$1,025 is based on a SOTP valuation methodology, 
including (1) 3.5x blended 2028E P/B for ABF & BT substrate, (2) 20x 2028E P/E for 
high-growth AI server/switch related PCB (in line with industry average), and (3) 8x 
2028E P/E for low-growth consumer electronics PCB (in line with industry downcycle 
average valuation). 
Key downside risks include: (1) weaker-than-expected iPhone demand or slower 
content upgrade, (2) delay in new substrate capacity or slower-than-expected yield rate 
ramp up progress, and (3) fiercer competition in China ABF market.

### Page 24

Investment Thesis - Kinsus
Kinsus is one of the key IC substrates suppliers globally with ~10% ABF/BT substrates
market share in 2024, with ~18% of total 2025 revenue contributed by its contact lens
subsidiary, Pegavision (6941.TW, Not Covered). With our view that the ABF substrate
shortage started from 2Q26 (ABF substrate return to supply demand balance from late
2025), we believe Kinsus’ performance will continue to improve compared with high-end

ABF peers considering the company’s increasing market share in the high-end ABF
substrate market; however, ~36% of the company’s revenue was contributed by BT
substrates in 2025 and 30% of the company’s ABF revenue is from non-LTA customers,
which we believe will continue to enjoy a better pricing outlook. As a result, we believe
Kinsus’ earnings outlook should continue to be strong (~118% 2026-29E CAGR), mainly
driven by better BT substrate and non-LTA ABF substrate pricing outlook, despite the

unfavorable demand outlook before 1H26. We believe Kinsus is an experienced
substrate supplier with a clear capacity expansion plan that can also upgrade its product
portfolio in the long term. We believe we are at the beginning of an industry upcycle,
making us positive on Kinsus’ stock performance over the next 12 months.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - Kinsus
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$1,515 for Kinsus is based on

19.5x 2028E P/E, which is +2.0x STDV higher than Kinsus’ past upcycle average P/E
(14.2x).
Key downside risks: (1) Slower-than-expected PC demand recovery, (2)
Slower-than-expected ABF substrates pricing upgrading outlook, (3)
Slower/Less-than-expected AI server orders from 2H26.
Investment Thesis - NYPCB
NYPCB manufactures and markets printed circuit boards (PCBs) and integrated circuit
(IC) substrates (including ABF and BT substrates). We are Buy rated on NYPCB

considering the company’s high exposure to BT substrates and non-LTA ABF substrate
(70%+ of total revenue), where we expect GM/OPM to increase significantly in the
coming quarters (from breakeven in OPM in 1H25 to 26/42% in 2026/27E) due to the
favorable substrate pricing outlook. Despite the company’s low exposure to high-end
ABF substrate industry (<20% of total revenue in 2025), we believe the expanding
demand from ASIC AI server and high-end switch IC demand will drive the company’s

high-end ABF revenue exposure to 40%+ by 2027E, suggesting better GM/OPM outlook
in the long term. In 2H25-2H26, we believe NYPCB will continue to benefit from stronger
ABF/BT pricing and should be a key beneficiary of the pricing uptrend considering the
company’s key focus on BT and non-LTA ABF substrate industries. Moreover, in the long
term (after 2026), we expect the ABF industry to return to supply tightness, and NYPCB’s
expanding high-end exposure will lead to a long-term earnings growth outlook (148%

2026-28E earnings CAGR). As a result, we see solid upside potential for NYPCB’s
earnings and stock performance.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - NYPCB
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$2,700 for NYPCB is based
on 21x 2028E P/E, which is +1.5x STDV higher than the past upcycle average P/E (13.3x).
Key downside risks: (1) Slower-than-expected PC demand recovery, (2)

Investment Thesis - Unimicron Technology
Unimicron is a key supplier of ABF substrates (25% market share in 2025) and BT
substrates and also produces FPC/HDI/PCB products. We are Neutral rated on the
shares given long-term growth opportunity prospects: (1) over 70% of ABF shipments
are covered by long-term agreements (LTA), which limits pricing flexibility and makes

price hike less likely; (2) the company’s execution track record has been weaker than
peers; (3) capacity expansion plan remains slower than peers, constraining its ability to
capture incremental AI demand. The current ABF substrates market is seeing more
favorable supply demand outlook, and Unimicron is likely to see better pricing outlook
to reflect rising material costs in the coming quarters. AI ABF account for 10% of the
company’s total revenue, but the growth is likely to be slower than peers due to the

company continuing to lose market share to peers given its less favourable production
yield and slow capacity expansion. Overall, we are Neutral rated on Unimicron given our
outlook for limited upside potential, barring ASIC AI servers, despite our generally
positive view in the long term on its profitability trajectory.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - Unimicron Technology
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$1,270 for Unimicron is

based on 17.4x 2028E P/E, which is 2.7x STDV higher than the past upcycle average P/E
(10.3x).
Key upside/downside risks: (1) Slower/faster-than-expected PC demand recovery, (2)
Slower/faster-than-expected ABF substrates upgrading pace, and (3) Slower/faster
than-expected AI server PCB market share loss progress.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Update
Unimicron  | Asia Pacific
Key ABF substrate customer
announces capacity reservation
agreements
What's new? According to  a press release on October 21, at GTC Berlin NVIDIA
(covered by Joseph Moore) named Unimicron as one of  three partners in its plan to
lift substrate capacity 2.5x over three years. A capacity reservation agreement
supports Unimicron’s KF (Kuangfu) plant expansion in Taiwan, with additions
expected from 2Q27 and full capacity by mid-2028.

Our read-through: We believe this implies robust multi-year, AI-driven substrate
demand. NVIDIA’s capacity reservation likely covers Unimicron's 2Q27 KF Plant 2
step flagged in July (see our earnings call takeaways), which could lower the
financial risk of overcapacity as Unimicron deploys its NT$53.7bn 2026 capex
budget. Stay OW with a price target of NT$1,775.

Update
2
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Unimicron (3037.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.2%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 14%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF substrate demand from PC and server customers

n Capex cuts; halt to its capacity expansion plan
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.,
CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition
n Yield issues or production hiccups when ramping new capacity

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

營收走勢圖

研究員/張均壕 JUN_HAO1@uni-psg.com

研究部仍看好時碩未來營運，主要是考量液冷散熱零件業務隨著客
戶訂單逐步放量及出貨規模擴大 ，帶動 2H26 業績大幅提升。 此外 ，
航太事業成長前景仍佳，工業事業亦可望持續增長，明年展望依舊
正向。研究部預估時碩 2026 年 EPS 3.52 元(+162%YoY)，2027 年
EPS 4.63 元(+32%YoY)。以明年獲利為評價基礎，給予 20 倍本益
比，目標價 93 元。

重點摘要：

1. 受惠AI趨勢，液冷散熱訂單將帶動3Q26業績強勁增長： 
時碩3Q26合併營收上升至18.18億元(+24%QoQ，+43%YoY)，主
要是受惠AI資料中心液冷相關零組件的貢獻增加，令7、8、9
月單月營收持續創新高 。考量營收擴大， 規模效應顯現， 預期
時碩3Q26毛利率將提升至 24.5%，營益率提升至 8.8%，預估
3Q26稅後淨利1.38億元(+101%QoQ，+34%YoY)，預估3Q26EPS 
1.26元。

2. 液冷散熱產品進入爬坡期，4Q26營運淡季不淡： 
展望4Q26， 雖然年底通常為時碩汽車、工業業務的淡季， 但受
惠散熱零件仍處在出貨爬坡期，預期季度營收與獲利仍有望
再墊高。研究部預估時碩 4Q26合併營收18.85億元(+4%QoQ，
+55%YoY)，4Q26稅後淨利1.40億元(+1%QoQ，+113%YoY)， 預估
4Q26EPS 1.27元 。 全年度來看 ， 研究部預估時碩2026年合併營
收將提升至65.25億元(+31%YoY)，年度毛利率24.1%，營益率
7.1%，稅後淨利3.87億元(+161%YoY)，預估2026年EPS 3.52
元。

3. 航太業務維持成長動能，積極拓展新商機 
受惠於全球航太產業需求穩健及上游關鍵零組件產能吃緊，
時碩管理層持續看好航太業務前景 ，其中天陽航太營運成長
率預估可達15%～20%。 此外 ， 公司積極擴充泰國航太產能 ， 規
劃建置鋁合金及不鏽鋼生產線，以掌握全球航太供應鏈重組
及非紅供應鏈商機 ， 中長期目標為貢獻約10億元營收 。 除既有
航太業務外，時碩亦積極拓展人形機器人及半導體設備等新
興應用領域。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

在人形機器人方面， 時碩已投入諧波減速器及關節零組件開
發，並與國際及中國大陸主要廠商展開合作。半導體設備方
面，時碩持續開發相關精密零組件，未來將配合泰國新廠布
局 ， 爭取台灣客戶海外工廠的供應商機 。 整體而言 ， 隨著航太
業務穩健成長，以及人形機器人與半導體設備等新領域逐步
拓展 ， 將有助於公司建立更多元的營運成長動能 ， 降低對傳統
汽車產業的依賴。

4. 明年業績續升，預估2027年EPS 4.63元： 
展望2027年 ， 考量日系大廠的液冷散熱訂單將延續至明年 ， 而
且還有美系散熱大廠客戶訂單與磁浮壓縮機關鍵訂單等待發
酵 ， 加上航太業務仍具偏強增速 ， 預期時碩2027年業績將持續
提升。研究部預估時碩2027年合併營收74.20億元(+14%YoY)，
年度毛利率 25.0%， 預估2027年EPS將進一步提升至 4.63元。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

9月经济数据快报
前三季度国内生产总值1071071亿元，按不变价格计算，同比增长4.6%。分产业看，第一产业
增加值58994亿元，同比增长3.7%；第二产业增加值383822亿元，增长3.8%；第三产业增加值
628255亿元，增长5.2%。分季度看，一季度国内生产总值同比增长5.0%，二季度增长4.3%，三
季度增长4.5%。从环比看，三季度国内生产总值增长1.2%。

前三季度，全国规模以上工业增加值同比增长5.3%。分三大门类看，采矿业增加值同比增长
2.0%，制造业增长5.7%，电力、热力、燃气及水生产和供应业增长5.4%。装备制造业增加值同比
增长10.2%，高技术制造业增加值增长14.3%，分别快于全部规模以上工业增加值4.9和9.0个百分
点。
前三季度，全国固定资产投资（不含农户）334115亿元，同比下降7.2%，与1—8月份持平；扣
除房地产开发的固定资产投资下降4.2%。其中，知识产权产品投资同比增长9.3%。高技术产业投
资同比增长4.9%。分产业看，第一产业投资同比下降3.9%，第二产业投资下降2.7%，第三产业投
资下降10.0%。
前三季度，货物进出口总额399399亿元，同比增长18.8%。其中，出口231979亿元，增长
16.4%；进口167420亿元，增长22.3%。对共建“一带一路”国家进出口增长17.1%。民营企业进
出口增长19.1%，占进出口总额的比重为56.9%。机电产品出口增长23.7%，占出口总额的比重为

宏观金融团队 范丽军 Z0018336

 상대 (3.2) 1.6  63.7   
 절대 (달러환산) 0.3  (11.5) 239.5

3Q26 전사 예상 영업이익 100조원 
DS부문 예상 영업이익은 100.0조원(OPM 67.4%, YoY +1538.5%, QoQ +14.5%) 으로, 
달러환율 1,380원기준, 메모리와 비메모리 사업부문 예상 영업손익은 각각 102.0조원,    
-2.0조원으로 추정한다. 1) 메모리반도체 가격 상승세가 이어지는 가운데, HBM4 판매 
본격화에 따른 영향을 고려하면 DRAM/NAND 분기 평균 가격 상승폭은 각각 +18.0%, 
+16.0%으로 추정하고, 2)비메모리반도체 사업 적자는 지속될 전망이기 때문이다. 다만 
특별 성과급 지급에 따른 충당금 영향을 제외하고 선단공정 중심의 파운드리 가동률을 
고려하면 적자폭은 유의미하게 감소한 것으로 파악된다는 점이 긍정적이다.   
SDC부문 예상 영업이익은 1.1조원(OPM 13.0%, YoY -9.6%, QoQ +54.9%)으로, 1)국내 
주력 고객사향 폴더블 스마트폰 판매 호조가 사업부의 외형 성장에 긍정적으로 작용했을 
것으로 추정됨에도 불구하고, 2) 8.6세대 IT OLED 패널 양산 개시에 따른 감가상각비(약 
0.3조원 수준)와 북미 고객 중심의 판가 인하 압박 영향으로 전년동기대비 수익성은 약 
2%pt 훼손이 불가피할 전망이다. DX부문 예상 영업손익은 -1.1조원(YoY 적자전환, QoQ 
적자지속)으로, 메모리반도체를 포함한 IT 부품 가격 부담이 전분기대비 확대되고 있기 
때문이다. MX와 VD/DA사업부 예상 손익은 각각 -0.9조원, -0.2조원으로 추정한다.

투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 63만원으로 상향 조정  
메모리반도체의 가격 상승폭이 다소 둔화되겠지만, 2027~2028년에도 타이트한 수급 
환경이 이어질 전망이라는 점을 주목한다. 1)시장은 신규 증설과 HBM 관련 노이즈로 인한 
2028년 피크아웃 가능성을 우려하고 있으나, HBM 의 범용 DRAM 생산능력 잠식과 AI 
추론 확산에 따른 고용량 메모리 수요를 감안하면 공급 제약의 해소 시점은 예상보다 
늦어질 가능성이 높기 때문이다. 뿐만 아니라, 2)메모리 업사이클이 장기화될 경우 동사 
FCF는 과거대비 구조적으로 확대되어 보다 친화적인 주주환원 정책(2027~2029년, 
2027년 1월 예정)을 제시할 가능성도 배제할 수 없기 때문이다. 동사 2027년 연간 실적 
추정치 상향 조정에 따라 목표주가를 63만원(2027년 BPS * Target PBR 3.5배 적용)으로 
상향 조정한다. 피크아웃 우려에 따른 주가 조정은 중장기 관점에서 매수 기회다.

Appendix

Document 230: AI行情震荡，去香港金融科技周听听掌舵者怎么说

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI行情震荡，去香港金融科技周听听掌舵者怎么说.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-23
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

华尔街⻅闻 2026 ⾹港⾦融科技周深度调研
摘要：
AI投资这笔账，⾦融巨头怎么看？
营收和利润都超出预期，股价却跌了近 7% 。
10 ⽉ 22 ⽇， Meta 财报后的市场反应，再次显露出投资者对 AI 投⼊的担忧。持续增加的⽀出、庞
⼤的融资需求，让市场重新审视这场投资竞赛。
巨额投⼊还在继续，资⾦能否持续跟上，回报⼜将在什么时候出现？
研究这笔账，需要把技术进展与商业需求放在⼀起看。科技公司准备投⼊多少，⾦融机构怎样
评估⻛险，客⼾愿意为哪些应⽤付费，共同影响着这轮产业扩张能⾛多远。
在 10 天后开幕的⾹港⾦融科技周上，有两场讨论值得带着这些疑问去听。
⼀场追问 “AI 变便宜后，谁能盈利？ ” 耐能创始⼈兼 CEO 刘峻诚、汇丰集团创投负责⼈ Moran
Levinovitz 等，将探讨 AI 成本下降后，产业链上的利润如何分配。
另⼀场聚焦⾦融 AI 的规模化应⽤与商业化。⾹港投资推⼴署助理署⻓梁瀚璟、⻨肯锡⾹港管理
合伙⼈ Arthur Shek 等，将讨论 AI 如何转化为业务收益，以及应⽤中的责任边界。

11 ⽉ 2 ⽇⾄ 3 ⽇，⼤会主论坛将在⾹港会议展览中⼼举⾏。今年的嘉宾还包括⾹港特区⾏政⻓官
李家超、财政司司⻓陈茂波、⾹港⾦管局总裁余伟⽂，以及汇丰、渣打集团 CEO 等。政策制定
者、⾦融机构负责⼈和科技企业经营者的讨论，为投资者提供了相互参照的视⻆。
华尔街⻅闻此次的港深调研，也将沿着这些问题展开：11⽉ 1 ⽇⾄ 5 ⽇，从两天⼤会，到⾹港⾦
融机构交流，再到深圳企业参访，把会场⾥的观点与具体业务放在⼀起看。

听政策⽅向：看清创新进⼊市场的条件
⾹港⾦融科技周曾是重要政策的发布现场。 2025 年，⾦管局在会上公布 “ ⾦融科技 2030” ，将数
据与⽀付基础设施、⾦融机构应⽤ AI 等列为重点⽅向。
因此，今年到现场听监管者的讨论，可以继续关注：哪些⽅向获得⽀持，推进需要哪些配套，
机构还⾯临什么实际障碍。对研究科技与⾦融⾏业的⼈来说，这些信息有助于理解⼀项创新从
技术⽅案进⼊商业应⽤的过程。
听机构判断：了解资本与业务需求如何变化
今年⼤会的三⼤引擎是⾦融 + 、 AI+ 、出海。议程还安排了全球资本投向、 AI 与财富管理，以及
企业全球化与跨境⽀付等讨论。
同⼀项技术，在不同参与者眼中可能有不同的价值。科技公司强调产品能⼒，银⾏关注可靠性
与业务效率，资管机构关⼼它将怎样影响企业的竞争⼒与盈利。

⾹港连接内地产业与国际⾦融市场，也让这些讨论具有更⼴的参照范围。关注科技投资，可以

Document 304: Global Strategy Views_ Competition for Capital

Source PDF: all report pdf/Global Strategy Views_ Competition for Capital.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-09-17
Pages: 12
Status: ok

### Page 1

1. There are two themes dominating our investor conversations: the impact of AI and 
the rise in interest rates. The two issues are linked as demand for capital from both 
the private and public sectors increase. In the private sector a surge in capex 
spending to fund AI infrastructure has eaten into free cash flow and forced 
companies to raise more in debt and equity markets. Meanwhile, government 
borrowing needs have increased as priorities shift towards upgrading critical 
infrastructure, energy security and defense at a time when cyclical inflationary 
pressures driven by higher energy prices are also resulting in higher policy rates. The 
combination has pushed up the cost of capital. As recently as 2022, for example, 
30-year bond yields in Germany and Japan were close to zero (Exhibit 1). A rise in 
yields, together with more uncertainty (over geopolitics and the future impact of AI) 
have, in combination, pushed up the cost of capital.

2. This rise in yields comes after a near-record period of equity outperformance 
relative to bonds over 10-year holding periods (Exhibit 2).

Exhibit 1: Rising yields and uncertainty have increased the cost of capital 
30-year government bond benchmark yields (%) 
          













### Page 2

As equities have continued to outperform bonds, equity risk premia have fallen back to 
levels last seen in the late 1990s, leaving equity markets more vulnerable to further 
increases in bond yields. That said, in the US and Japan, the ERP has bounced off recent 
lows (Exhibit 3).

3. The impact of rising bond yields, however, depends not only on the level of yields, but 
also on the reasons as well as the speed of the adjustment (Exhibit 4). It is the speed 
issue that has caused the biggest impact in the recent past. As our US strategists note, 
stocks have usually generated positive returns alongside rising interest rates unless the 
pace of rising rates exceeded 2+ standard deviations and this is a pattern that is broadly

yields would equate to about 50bps over a month or 30bps over two weeks, similar to 
the adjustments in recent days which explains the pullback in equity prices.

4. But despite the rise in yields, nominal GDP and profit growth remain strong and, until 
recently, the impact of rising bond yields has been offset by the strength in corporate 
profits. Earnings growth has been the main driver of equity returns over the past 18 
months in all regions (Exhibit 5). This strength has meant that PE multiples have either 
been flat (Japan and Europe) or have fallen (the US, Asia and EM).

In the US, in particular, the forward PE multiple for the S&P has come down from 22x at

### Page 4

the start of the year to 19x, in line with its long-run average, despite the market being 
close to its all-time high. 
5. While US equities have driven the bulk of equity outperformance for much of the 
fifteen years post the financial crisis, the improvement in earnings across regions, and 
the sharper de-rating in the US, has resulted in equity performance being 
broader-based by geography. Since 2025, the US equity market has been the weakest of 
the major regions (Exhibit 6), a turnaround from the dominant trend since the financial 
crisis and increasing investor returns from a more geographically diversified portfolio.

6. Supportive profit growth has been accompanied by positive earnings revisions, with 
2026 and 2027 estimates moving higher across major regions. There are four broad 
areas which have been driving much of this profit growth. First, technology earnings 
continue to be very robust. Second, rising energy prices have pushed up profits in the 
commodity sector. Third, banks have generally enjoyed strong earnings backed by 
positive nominal GDP growth, steep yield curves and strong private sector balance 
sheets. Finally, sectors such as Industrials have benefited from the surge in AI capex 
spending which has spilled over into improved revenues for the ‘pick and shovels’ of AI 
infrastructure. The breadth of the earnings’ growth has also increased the opportunity 
for investors to diversify across sectors as well as countries.

Exhibit 6: US equities have underperformed other major regions since 2025

7. While technology profit growth has remained strong, the surge in capex spending 
among the hyperscalers has increasingly eaten through their free cash flow (Exhibit 8) 
prompting companies to look for alternative sources of funding, in the credit and equity 
markets.  The capex spending growth for AA-rated issuers has been substantial: the 65% 
year-over-year growth in Q2 marks the 10th consecutive quarter that aggregate AA 
capex growth exceeds 35%. They have also turned to the convertible bond market 
where volume has also increased year to date reaching $135 billion in the US, with 
AI-related borrowers driving 44% of total issuance, on our estimates. 
Our Credit team raised their full-year US IG gross issuance forecast by $200bn to $2.3 
trillion and AI-related issuers account for a quarter of USD IG gross supply this year, 
pushing overall supply to a year-to-date record.

The combination of higher cost of capital and greater capital intensity in the tech sector 
has reduced the value of their future cash flows and triggered a de-rating in the biggest 
companies.  The dominant capex hyperscalers in the US now have a forward PE close to 
the average of the rest of the market.

8. Software companies have also de-rated on the back of fears of disruption from AI 
while memory and chips stocks have also experienced lower multiples as their earnings

### Page 7

surge. Globally technology has seen its PE ratio moderate, falling below its 20-year 
median (Exhibit 10). Meanwhile, the spending of these companies has spilled over into 
better earnings and more visible cash flows in many other parts of the economy. 
Healthcare, Consumer Discretionary and Industrials are all more expensive in absolute 
terms than IT, and Industrials has re-rated and now has a PE close to the 90th percentile 
of its 20-year range.

9. For these reasons, we have argued that Technology equities have not experienced a 
classic bubble because valuation levels are much lower than in previous financial 
bubbles. Nevertheless, the risk for investors is less about valuation and more whether 
there is an unsustainable earnings bubble. Bubbles in earnings, rather than valuations, 
have occurred in previous periods.  The Bank sector, for example, briefly became the 
biggest sector in the S&P 500 in the run up to the financial crisis of 2008/09 (Exhibit 11). 
Unlike the technology bubble of the late 1990s, or the Japanese bubble of the late 
1980s, Bank stocks did not experience a major valuation bubble at the time, but the 
surge in earnings that drove the sector’s outperformance proved to be unsustainable.

### Page 8

There are, however, important differences to the Tech sector today. The earnings of the 
banks were boosted by sharply rising leverage and the asset that they were financing – 
US real estate – did experience a valuation bubble. When the housing market collapsed, 
pushing economies into recession, it triggered a sharp fall in underlying earnings for the 
banking sector. There are important differences today. First, technology profits remain 
very robust, and balance sheets are strong overall. Second, interest coverage ratios for 
the aggregate S&P 500 and its median stock, for example, rank in the 99th and 68th 
percentiles relative to the past 20 years. Third, demand for compute is accelerating and 
outstripping supply; Microsoft recently declared their intent to triple data centre 
capacity in 6 years. The bottlenecks are increasingly power, land and data centre 
capacity rather than a lack of compute demand. At our annual Communacopia 
Technology Conference, Nvidia reiterated its 2030 AI TAM outlook to $3tn-$4tn and 
supply constraints continue to be broad based. Nonetheless, any slowdown in profit 
growth, in an environment of a much higher cost of capital, could put downward 
pressure on equity prices, reducing confidence in future cash flows across the ecosystem 
from the hyperscalers to the ‘pick and shovels’ that have been benefiting from the capex 
boom.  
10. Given the near-term risks in bond markets, we remain neutral on equities, alongside 
other asset classes on a 3-month horizon, but continue to be overweight equities over 
12 months. While profit growth is likely to slow from the recent strong pace, ongoing 
growth in nominal GDP should underpin equity markets and the earnings cycle 
propelling equity returns even without any valuation expansion from current levels and 
our economists continue to see a low risk of recession. As our asset allocation team has 
shown, earnings growth and ROE are likely to continue to be the key drivers of equity 
performance relative to bonds, and we expect these fundamentals to remain supportive 
for equity returns. Nevertheless, we continue to recommend well diversified equity 
exposure as opportunities continue to broaden across geographies, sectors and factors.

Stock correlations are at multi-year lows across the major equity markets (Exhibit 12).

Even within sectors like technology, correlations have fallen sharply (Exhibit 13). The 
‘magnificent seven’ that was long seen as a homogeneous group of technology winners 
has also seen significant dispersion of returns. Differentiation, diversification and alpha 
generation are likely to remain the key drivers of equity returns. With higher bond yields, 
valuations are not likely to rise making earnings the critical driver of returns for investors.

### Page 1

Paris, 23 October 2026

EcoFlash

Q3 Growth in advanced economies: solid despite shocks

Growth looks to have held up in Q3 despite successive shocks (see our analysis), as it did in 2025 and H1 2026. A longer 
Middle East conflict has fed into more persistent inflation; drought has worsened in many countries, and interest rates 
have risen further – at the short end, with both the ECB and the Fed raising policy rates in September, and even more at 
the long end. However, our nowcasts show that growth is bending without breaking in most advanced economies. GDP 
figures (due on 29 October in the US and 30 October for the euro area and its member states) are expected to confirm 
that. In the Euro area, momentum looks stable (excluding Ireland, whose GDP is volatile). In Germany, growth has been 
uninterrupted since Q4 2025, while France is likely to have returned to growth after stagnating in H1. In the US, growth 
is likely to have gathered pace, according to the GDP Now, still driven by AI, but increasingly by other sectors as well.

Euro area: resilience beneath the surface. Our nowcast points to growth slowing to 0.3% in q/q, though the pace is stable 
excluding Ireland (Irish GDP is volatile and had markedly boosted the Q2 figure). Excluding Ireland, growth should hold at its H2 
average, a sign of the bloc’s underlying resilience. Also, momentum has broadened to a growing number of sectors ( see our 
analysis). While industrial production stalled over the summer, services remained buoyant. The services PMI rebounded sharply 
in Q3 and sits firmly in expansion territory (53.0 in September). Improving job-related survey data (PMI and European Commission 
surveys) and a stable unemployment rate (6.4% in August) hint at more hiring in Q3 – a support for household consumption that 
partly offsets purchasing-power losses from persistent inflation. Corporate investment is underpinned by buoyant new investment 
lending (8.7% y/y In August, 12-month cumulative). 
France: growing, nevertheless. The succession of shocks does not appear to have prevented France from returning to 
growth in Q3, at 0.2% q/q, according to our nowcast. One-off factors accounted for the stall in activity in H1. In Q3, the drought 
worsened and industrial production remained subdued. However, market services production rebounded in July and August, 
notably in business services, restaurants, digital and real estate. The latter points to residential investment recovering after a weak 
H1. Corporate investment likely followed, as reflected in buoyant credit flows and AI-related investment. Household consumption 
is likely to have improved slightly on higher electric vehicle sales.  
Euro area  France

Source: Refinitiv, BNP Paribas  Source: Refinitiv, BNP Paribas

Germany: growth still solid. The H1 rebound in activity is moderating but continues. Our nowcast points to a fourth straight 
quarter of growth in Q3, at 0.2% q/q (after 0.3% in Q4 2025, 0.4% in Q1 2026, and 0.3% in Q2). Manufacturing activity eased 
slightly, according to the September PMI survey (53.9, down 0.4 m/m). Government spending will remain growth-supportive, with 
defence and infrastructure outlays gaining traction. Infrastructure spending continues to underpin activity in public works, which 
stands at its highest since November 2021 (2.5% 3m/3m in August). Exports (4.4% y/y between January and August) remain the 
main growth engine, helped by a pivot towards Europe (7.2%). 
United States: an expansion undeterred by inflation. Growth is likely to have gathered pace in Q3. The Atlanta Fed’s GDP 
Now points to a 3.5% annualized growth rate (AR), close to our own 3.7% forecast (0.9% non -AR) and up from 2.2% in 
Q2. Domestic demand remains the engine. Consumption is weathering the pickup in inflation, while the AI-driven capex cycle 
remains robust – as reflected in retail sales and nondefence capital-goods orders (excl. aircraft). As a result, growth in final sales

### Page 2

to private domestic purchasers 1 should print above 4% AR again. By contrast, external trade should remain a drag on growth, 
reflecting AI-related import needs. Survey data point to mome ntum that is both solid and broad -based. The ISM indices have 
remained firmly in growth territory (54.9 quarterly average for manufacturing and services alike). At the same time, they highlight 
persistent price and supply-chain pressures.

Germany   United States

Source: Refinitiv, BNP Paribas   Source: Refinitiv, Atlanta Fed, BNP Paribas

《東協尚未擺脫整理，美印關係改善訊號浮現》
9月MSCI東協指數下跌 2.6%，雖然全球公債殖利率
與油價走升帶來評價面壓力 ，但從股價跌幅及外資
賣超規模來看 ，本波修正仍偏向短線漲多後的技術
性調節。修正後，東協股市本益比已由高於近兩年
平均2倍標準差回落至約 1倍標準差 ，RSI亦降至40
以下，顯示市場過熱情況明顯改善 。然而在聯準會
升息及殖利率維持高檔的背景下 ，若基本面未能提
供更多上修空間 ，預期股市短期仍將維持震盪整理
格局。印度方面，9月回檔已使股價回測 3月低點，
本益比降至 19.9倍，RSI跌破30，顯示短線賣壓已

有過度反應。此外，莫迪於9月金磚峰會公開反對去
美元化，近期川普亦表態支持印歐經濟走廊 ，反映
美印經貿關係出現改善訊號 。後續可持續關注雙邊
合作進展及其對印度中長期投資影響。

《外資9月轉為小幅賣超，呼應8月轉中立看法》
8月下調東協評等至中立 ，主要考量 6至8月股市累
積漲幅已大致反映第三季經濟維持穩健成長的預期 ，
在缺乏新的催化劑下 ，市場進一步上行空間受限 。
進入9月後，美國公債殖利率與國際油價同步走升，
使市場重新評估高利率與成本壓力對企業獲利的影
響，並加大非科技類股及非科技市場的修正幅度 。

力，東協股市 9月僅下跌 2.6%，跌幅仍屬溫和；從
資金面來看 ，外資雖轉為淨賣超 ，但賣超規模明顯
低於第二季調整期間 ，顯示本波修正較偏向漲多後
的獲利了結與資產重新配置 ，而非反映市場對區域
基本面的明顯轉弱或出現恐慌性撤資。

《出口支撐成長敘事未變 ，但高通膨也比預期持久 》
東協及印度經濟仍展現韌性，多數國家8月出口維持
雙位數成長且高於長期平均 ，與全球製造業擴張趨
勢相符 ，其中新加坡及馬來西亞受惠 AI強勁資本支
出，8月出口成長年增率逾 5成，顯示外需仍是重要
成長支撐 。強勁的出口與製造活動不僅有助支撐企
業獲利 ，也在一定程度上抵銷高利率對消費與投資
的壓力 。另一方面 ，印尼、菲律賓等先前高通膨國
家雖已進入降溫趨勢 ，但通膨回落速度仍較緩慢 ，
使央行在降息時程上維持審慎 。整體而言 ，目前東
協與印度景氣尚未出現明顯轉弱 ，但高利率環境可
能維持較市場預期更久 ，意味短期經濟成長動能將
更仰賴外需及製造業活動的支撐。

《川普表態支持印歐經濟走廊 ，重構印度樞紐角色 》
川普近期表態支持印歐經濟走廊 (IMEC)，使這項拜
登任內支持，但因加薩戰爭與區域衝突而停滯近3年
的計畫再度受到市場關注 。隨著中東地緣風險升溫 ，
紅海及荷姆茲海峽等關鍵航道不確定性提高 ，各國
對替代性能源與運輸路徑的需求同步增加 。從規劃
路線來看 ，印歐經濟走廊以印度為起點 ，串連海灣
產油國與歐洲市場 ，若計畫順利推進 ，除有助印度
取得更穩定的中東能源供給 、降低對俄羅斯石油的
依賴外 ，也將進一步鞏固其作為全球製造與供應鏈
樞紐的地位 。對印股而言 ，IMEC有望同時強化能源
安全與供應鏈地位 ，鞏固印度在全球製造的地位 ，
並為基建、工業及製造類股帶來中長期題材支撐。

化能源安全 、降低對俄油依
賴，並鞏固全球製造與供應
鏈樞紐地位。

 【粤开宏观】全球 K 型分化的三大驱
动：AI、利率与债务

近期报告

何未充分释放 ？——中国文旅消费的特
征、问题与制度成因》2026-10-07

摘要 
“K 型经济”概念在新冠疫情后兴起，但并未随疫情结束而消失，反而伴随
人工智能发展带动的经济结构分化而再度被反复讨论。经济结构的分化是普
遍的，均衡是例外、分化是常态，不同行业有快慢之分、不同群体有贫富之
别，但是当前的新变化是新一轮技术革命以前所未有的速度在加剧供需之间、
行业之间、群体之间和地域之间的四重分化，甚至影响到经济总量的走势以
及制约经济的进一步发展。 
当前， “K 型分化”已逐渐演变为全球性的经济特征，主要体现在：中国和欧
洲经济均呈现明显的行业分化，美国则更突出表现为群体分化，日韩及亚洲
新兴市场大多表现为内外需分化。这些共同点在于，全球增长越来越依赖少
数部门、企业和人群，宏观经济的稳定性建立在越来越窄的基础之上 ，带动
全球经济进入 “低就业增长” 状态。本文主要讨论全球经济 K 型分化的特征、
原因及影响，这属于结构层面分析，对全球经济总量及经济周期的影响 后续
另行撰文分析。 
全球 K 型分化的形成并非偶然，而是 AI 技术革命、利率环境和债务周期三
重力量叠加的结果。 一是AI 技术革命引发资源再分配， 资源集中涌入新兴行
业、流出传统行业，从源头上催生分化；二是统一的利率产生非对称影响，
对少部分主体而言是“锦上添花” ，对多数主体则“雪上加霜” ，进一步放大
分化；三是疫情后政府债务高企、利率易上难下，财政空间与经济托底能力
受约束，导致分化逐步固化。 
K 型分化正重塑全球经济运行逻辑，深刻影响全球增长韧性、金融市场与宏
观政策框架。其一，增长层面，全球经济增长更加依赖少数部门，韧性下降、
脆弱性上升。其二，资产层面，全球债券利率上行、股市结构分化，金融市
场波动加剧，投资者获得稳定收益的难度加大。其三，政策层面，宏观政策
框架亟需调整，结构性和总量政策需加强协同。K 型分化讲的是结构层面的
问题，结构分析不能代替总量分析、结构政策也不能代替总量政策， 运用结
构性政策精准调控的同时，总量政策也不能缺位。总量承压与结构分化并不
矛盾，核心就在于 AI 等新动能发展仍难以弥补房地产等旧动能的拖累力量。
因此，宏观政策在总量上仍需给予足够支持，保持总需求基本稳定，为转型
营造适宜的宏观环境。

风险提示：全球 AI 投资及发展速度不及预期，全球货币紧缩超预期， 全球金
融市场波动超预期等。

一、K 型分化已成为全球经济现象
“K 型经济”概念于新冠疫情之后兴起，最初用以描述经济复苏的不均衡现象，即
一部分行业和群体迅速复苏，比如率先复工的大型企业及虚拟经济部门、受益于资产价
格上涨的高收入家庭等；另一部分则持续落后，比如接触型服务业和中低收入群体受冲
击更久。 但随着各国经济逐步从疫情状态回归常态， 分化并未得到根本性缓解。 近年来，
伴随长期利率中枢抬升、通胀粘性凸显，叠加人工智能（AI）技术革命出现，K 型分化
被进一步放大，并逐渐演变为全球性经济特征。
从全球范围看， 本轮K 型分化在各主要经济体均有不同程度的体现， 只是分化形态
各有侧重。中国的分化主要源于行业间分化，内嵌于新旧动能转换进程，新动能加快成
长但旧动能收缩，当然行业分化亦会导致不同企业和群体表现分化。美国则更突出表现
为群体间分化，AI 浪潮推动资本市场繁荣与高收入群体消费强劲，而中低收入群体面临
高通胀、高利率的双重挤压，这一特征已被广泛讨论。欧洲的分化表现为产业和国家两

个层面，产业层面新能源和数字化产业扩张较快，传统制造业和高耗能产业持续承压；
国家层面 “欧洲火车头” 德国在传统能源冲击的影响下制造业持续下滑并拖累经济增长，
2024-2025 年德国制造业增加值（不变价）累计下降 5.2%，南欧国家如西班牙、意大利
等增长反而好于核心国家。
日韩及亚洲新兴市场大多呈现明显的“外强内弱”分化。日本半导体制造设备、AI
相关零部件出口保持高增长，头部科技企业盈利改善，但国内消费复苏乏力，中小企业
景气度持续低迷； 韩国半导体与AI 产业链拉动出口表现强劲， 财阀企业资本开支与盈利
规模持续扩大，但房地产市场深度调整、内需消费疲软，中小企业经营压力显著上升，
财阀与中小企业的分化不断加剧。 越南上半年经济增速创多年新高，实际 GDP 同比大幅
增长 8.2%，但增长主要由外资企业和受扶持的头部企业贡献，宏观高增长与普通民众获
得感之间存在落差。
总体来看，尽管各国分化的具体表现与成因有所差异，但共同特征清晰：全球经济
增长越来越依赖少数部门、少数企业和少数人群，宏观经济的稳定建立在越来越窄的基

础之上，这内在地导致全球经济增长的脆弱性。这是本轮 K 型分化区别于传统周期分化
的核心，也是理解当前全球经济运行的关键。
二、全球 K 型分化的三大驱动因素：AI、利率与债务
全球 K 型分化的形成并非偶然， 而是技术革命、 金融环境与债务周期等三重力量叠
加的结果，具有深刻的全球共性。
（一）AI 技术革命引发资源再分配，催生分化
AI 引领新一轮技术和产业革命，长期有望显著提升生产率与企业盈利，这一乐观预
期驱动资本、 人才、 技术等要素持续向AI 相关领域集中， 资源从传统行业流向新兴行业，
从低效率部门流向高效率部门，从根源上催生了结构分化。
行业发展分化衍生出增长与就业分化、实体与金融分化、宏观与微观背离等一系列
分化。AI 相关产业资本密集、技术密集，而非劳动密集，对 GDP 的拉动显著，但对就
业的带动作用显著低于传统部门， 导致产出强、 就业弱， 全球进入 “低就业增长” 状态。
产业升级与就业吸纳的不同步，是全球共同面临的结构性难题。全球资本市场均提前定
价 AI 产业红利， 科技板块大幅上涨， 而传统行业估值持续承压， 资本市场的分化成为实

体经济分化的提前映射与放大。宏观层面 GDP、工业生产、出口等总量数据表现尚可，

### Page 4

宏观深度 
请务必阅读最后特别声明与免责条款 www.ykzq.com  4 / 10 
但居民就业、收入、消费的实际体感偏弱，中小企业经营压力较大。总量平均数据掩盖
了结构分化下不同群体的差异化感受，形成宏观数据与微观体感的反差。 
需要指出的是，与以往技术革命相比，AI 技术进步速度更快，影响更具颠覆性，且
叠加大国战略竞争，企业“军备竞赛”更为普遍。这使得资源集中的速度更快、分化的
程度更深，且更容易形成自我强化的趋势，而非传统技术革命中渐进式的产业扩散。 
图表1：美国 AI 相关设备和软件投资大幅增长，其他投资增长逊色

资料来源：Wind、粤开证券研究院

（二）统一利率产生非对称的影响，放大分化 
利率水平由经济周期和宏观调控所决定。在 K 型分化的特殊环境下，统一的政策利
率和随之锚定的市场利率，对不同主体产生截然不同的影响。同样的利率，对 K 型上行
端实际是宽松的，对下行端实际是紧缩的，最终加剧分化。 
以美国为例，美联储于 2026 年 9 月重启加息， 政策利率上调至3.75-4.00%，是2023
年以来首次加息。10 月初，10 年期美债收益率突破 5.3%，刷新 2002 年以来新高。当前
美国政策利率显著高于 3%左右的长期利率水平， 但其是否具备管控通胀所需的 “限制性”
存在争议， 因其对不同主体的影响显著不同： 高利率不会明显抑制AI 科技投资， 现金流
充足的企业和家庭还可能受益于利息收入增长， 可谓“锦上添花” ； 而对于房地产等利率
敏感型行业、依赖银行信贷的中小企业、背负住房贷款与消费信贷的中低收入家庭，高
利率推高偿债成本，挤压经营与消费空间，可谓“雪上加霜” 。

对于中国而言，尽管我国政策和市场利率水平稳中有降，但考虑通胀因素后的实际
利率水平不低，分化效应同样存在。高技术产业更多依赖股权、债券等直接融资，在行
业高景气和良好发展前景的支撑下，各类资金持续流入，对利率相对不敏感，投资保持
较快增长；而对于房地产等传统行业，企业投资及居民购房主要依赖银行信贷，尽管贷
款利率处于历史低位，但房地产行业现金流持续偏弱、剔除通胀后的实际利率水平仍偏
高，实际金融条件可能依然偏紧，尚不足以扭转行业预期偏弱、房价总体承压的局面。

（三）全球债务可持续性不断恶化，固化本已存在的分化 
新冠疫情之后，全球主要经济体均推出大规模财政刺激政策，政府负债率阶梯式上
升。时至今日，经济虽已回归常态，但全球政府负债率普遍高于疫情前，化债成为全球
性难题。与此同时，全球AI 产业“军备竞赛”持续升温，各国政府纷纷加大研发与产业
扶持力度，进一步加大财政压力。据 IMF 预测，2026 年全球一般政府债务总额将突破
120 万亿美元，政府负债率将突破 95%，超过 2021 年、仅次于 2020 年。 
图表4：2020 年以来全球政府债务加快增长，政府负债率总体上升

资料来源：IMF（2026.4）、粤开证券研究院

全球债务可持续性担忧不断升温，投资者要求更高的风险补偿，推动国债利率中枢
上行；而利率上行又反过来加大政府付息压力，形成“债务高企、利率上行、付息压力
加大、债务更不可持续”的恶性循环。以美国为例，2026 财年的前 11 个月（2025 年 10
月至 2026 年 8 月），美国联邦政府利息支出累计首次突破 1 万亿美元，成为仅次于社会
保障的第二大联邦支出，超过国防等其他支出类别。美国政府债务压力成为推升中长期
限美债收益率的关键力量。欧元区债务压力亦同步上升，其中法国债务风险较为突出。
2026 年 8 月初以来，法国政府借贷成本已持续高于意大利、希腊等欧洲非核心国家；10
月以来，伴随法国债务规模攀升与财政脆弱性担忧加剧，10 年期法国国债收益率于 10
月 19 日升至 4.925%，创 2002 年以来新高。

图表5：债务可持续性担忧之下，海外主要经济体国债利率加快上行

全球债务高企，一方面使得全球利率水平偏高，持续对传统行业、中小企业和脆弱
群体形成压制；另一方面也约束了财政支出空间与效力，对脆弱主体的托底更加困难，
使得分化更难通过政策调节得到缓解，进而固化了 K 型分化的趋势。

三、全球 K 型分化的影响与应对 
K 型分化的持续深化， 正在重塑全球经济的运行逻辑， 并将深刻影响全球增长韧性、
金融市场与宏观政策框架。 
第一，全球经济增长更加依赖少数部门，脆弱性上升。当前全球经济的增长动力高
度集中于少数部门与少数主体，宏观稳定的基础持续收窄。例如，美国经济的韧性主要
依靠 AI 投资与股市财富效应支撑， 日韩及亚洲新兴市场的出口繁荣集中于半导体与 AI 
产业链，一旦上行部门的增长预期发生逆转，而下行部门尚未完成修复，经济将缺乏足
够的缓冲，表面的稳定可能在短时间内转为真实的下滑。同时，高利率对传统部门与脆
弱群体的压制长期存在，各国政府的财政空间被利息支出持续压缩，应对下一轮冲击的
政策余地不断缩小。增长看似韧性越强，其依赖的基础就越窄，背后的脆弱性就越值得
警惕。 
第二，全球金融市场波动加剧，债券利率上行是关键风险源。伴随各国政府债务增
长，市场对财政可持续性的担忧持续升温，债券市场的调整情绪在各国间相互传导，推
动主要经济体国债收益率中枢上移；叠加全球主要央行在通胀约束下重启加息，全球流
动性环境趋紧， 共同支撑利率维持高位。 全球股市虽有AI 产业景气支撑， 但内部分化同
样值得重视。美股市值和盈利向少数企业集中；A 股行业之间的分化也明显加大，科技
板块显著跑赢大盘。总的来看， 利率上行压缩估值， 股价结构分化加大选错方向的风险，
各类资产价格的波动性上升，投资者获得稳定收益的难度在加大。 
第三，宏观政策框架亟需调整，结构性和总量政策需加强协同。K 型分化下，经济
既非全面上行，也非全面衰退，传统的周期定位失灵，宏观数据与微观感受持续背离，
